# Geometry × dimension: finding

**Status: FINAL (2026-10-11).** Roadmap Stage 8 (`specs/naics-embedding-roadmap.md`).

Req 12 asked which geometry and which embedding dimension the text stage should use. Eight new
cells trained on five seeds each under Stage 7's settings, with only `model.geometry` and
`model.dimension` changed: hyperbolic at d = 8 and 32, and Euclidean and spherical at d = 8, 16 and
32. The saved configuration of all 40 runs differs in no other key (§1). The ninth cell, hyperbolic
at d = 16, is Stage 7's reference record with its ten seeds, and was not retrained. The outcome
validation MRR selected each run's earliest highest-MRR epoch, and the Mac then read each selected
checkpoint once on each of D8's three validation panels. No sealed test or outer split was opened.
The nine-cell decision (§3), under Stage 7's margins, chose `euclidean-d16`: the only
non-dominated arm, adopted over each of the other eight, including the reference.

## Sources

The canonical bundle is `stage3-supervision-v2/301cce28-539c-42ea-8781-496bbdcf511c`. The manifest
was passed explicitly to every bundle-reading campaign command. Canonical inputs were transported
and verified, and never rebuilt on Lambda. The hashes below identify the frozen inputs and the
dependency lock; every run's exported-table provenance agrees on the backbone, its revision, the
descriptions, the window summaries, the token window of 128 and the 2125 codes.

| Input | SHA-256 |
|---|---|
| Bundle `manifest.json` | 68545c669c4fdbc0de9958df6270ec7426994159da0bc119a0f1fd49177ef40f |
| `data/naics_descriptions.parquet` | fe8c54e36efb7470e46122c0071e16c03c3dba1c909073c84c91ec998a0fdc36 |
| Window summaries (export provenance) | dd425eb5ef9a7fa2be1b2e821c02f1b036f74f6ec503ff2fa70256ea7808a9a0 |
| `uv.lock` | 4167042e8a5a8caa9af62973151f681fffb50afaa1a7f6d1f801bd9e58bdac21 |
| `checkpoints/plan9_exit/text_only.parquet` | f4fb4f574f880c940ee8cf44c7f86615638e59f113b527d0b86973cd45ba32ff |

Plan 9's frozen text-only table is the comparator for every arm. D9 reduces it to each arm's
dimension at read time, so one table serves d = 8, 16 and 32. It is a frozen comparator, not a
trained arm, and it never supplies an arm's backbone revision.

| D9 field | Value |
|---|---|
| Backbone | sentence-transformers/all-MiniLM-L6-v2 |
| Backbone revision | 1110a243fdf4706b3f48f1d95db1a4f5529b4d41 |
| Descriptions SHA-256 | fe8c54e36efb7470e46122c0071e16c03c3dba1c909073c84c91ec998a0fdc36 |

| Table matrix fingerprint | 8941df995d7f1bfe39a79b0349a1771e73083e2cdda131b16344845210a1cbda |

The locked Mac environment reports torch 2.9.1, peft 0.17.1, polars 1.35.1 and transformers 4.57.1
in every export's provenance, and pytorch_lightning 2.5.5. `uv.lock` stayed at the hash above from
Task 10's pre-flight through the last decision, as the Stages 8–10 decisions that use Stage 7's
margins require.

**Source.** The public base, BASE, is `origin/main` at `eac0704f5cc137ca38745c454f4df033dec118ff`.
Two local sources trained the 40 runs, and the Source column of §1 names each run's:

- SOURCE, `7ef00c28c8405aa6d722d9d7c3a133f27f84e194`, trained the first 18 runs: seed 1 of all eight
  cells, seeds 2–5 of euclidean-d16 and spherical-d16, and seeds 2–3 of hyperbolic-d8. It is the
  local merge of `22df5e1` with BASE, not a replay, because published findings cite the private
  SHAs below.
- SOURCE 2, `4a1a3f264a70b5fdd3c7672f3f869d0fec4bc56a`, trained the other 22 runs. It is SOURCE
  plus one local commit that changes two files, `src/naics_embedder/remote/sync.py` (four lines)
  and `tests/unit/test_remote_sync.py` (one new regression test). It alters how the Mac verifies
  its cumulative pull baseline after a final pull (§1, deviation 3), and touches no model, loss,
  data, panel or training code.

The six private commits remain local on `main`:
`aa8ebd6098414ed9e956ef2df87713f0bb955d11`, `3fc580a81d839dc1fd4228d03abe43abf00b97bf`,
`d7927a56e82734701fcde60dc72ee832339f1093`, `5ce248f88dcb986ec09ee216f2eb2a9c605b0153`,
`b94ecf344cee5ae7f27c343b904455022cfde265` and `2d1f045dd88d44a0798918a81b43d09ab3ad782d`. Both
sources contain them. This finding branch starts from BASE and excludes them, so the real-instance
evidence qualifies the recorded private sources, as Stage 7's did, and does not establish
identical native readiness for the unchanged public base.

**Platform.** Training ran on two Lambda instances. Each reported logical CUDA 0 as NVIDIA
A100-SXM4-40GB, compute capability 8.0, 39.4935302734375 GB total memory and native BF16 support,
on driver 580.105.08, with one device. The backbone used `bf16-mixed`; fusion, projection, the
head, distances and losses stayed float32. Instance I1, `ubuntu@150.136.39.60`, trained the 18
SOURCE runs. Instance I2, `ubuntu@129.213.144.139`, trained the 22 SOURCE 2 runs. Launching
and terminating the instances was the user's action. Every export, regressor fit, store write and
decision read ran on the Mac.

**Margins.** Stage 7's `margins.json` fixed δ per panel at three sample standard deviations of
its ten reference seeds: outcome δ = 0.11869213794566072, regressor-seen δ = 0.0332953724957747
and regressor-held-out δ = 0.03675486585786976. They were fixed before any Stage 8 decision read
and were not changed.

## 1. The runs

Every run was fresh, used its seed once, and had a fixed 40-epoch budget with early stopping at a
patience of five epochs. The 21-key `run_settings` below are those of euclidean-d16 seed 1.
Across the 40 runs the saved configuration, which holds 24 flattened keys, differs in exactly one
of them, `run_settings.dimension`, which takes the value 8, 16 or 32 with the cell. The geometry
is not a configuration key; each run's geometry is recorded in its exported-table provenance and
in its arm record, and the provenance of all 40 runs names the geometry of its cell.

```json
{
  "fusion": "masked_mean",
  "dimension": 16,
  "radius_bound": 8.0,
  "code_code_weight": 1.0,
  "radial_weight": 1.0,
  "target_temperature": 1.0,
  "radial_step": 1.0,
  "logit_scale_init": 1.0,
  "logit_scale_range": [
    0.01,
    100.0
  ],
  "learning_rate": 0.0001,
  "weight_decay": 0.01,
  "warmup_epochs": 1,
  "lr_plateau_factor": 0.5,
  "lr_plateau_patience": 2,
  "early_stopping_patience": 5,
  "max_epochs": 40,
  "queries_per_step": 128,
  "accumulate_grad_batches": 1,
  "gradient_clip_val": 1.0,
  "accelerator": "cuda",
  "precision": "bf16-mixed"
}
```

Every run completed, with no resume. The "Stopped by" column says which rule ended it: the
40-epoch budget, or early stopping.

**What the epochs show.** All 15 runs of euclidean-d16, euclidean-d8 and hyperbolic-d8 used the
full 40 epochs and selected epochs 37–39, so the budget bound those arms, and their scores are
not converged values. The other 25 runs early-stopped after 7–11 epochs and selected epochs 1–5.
Every euclidean-d32 and spherical-d32 run selected epoch 1. This campaign did not investigate why
the d = 32 arms peak so early.

### Deviations from the plan

1. **Stale handoff references.** The handoff named `origin/main` `bc48a1a` and local `main`
   `22df5e1`; when the session started both had moved. BASE was `eac0704`, after docs PRs #132 and
   #133 (which touch `CLAUDE.md`, `README.md` and `tests/README.md` only), and local `main` was
   already `7ef00c2`, a parallel session's merge of `22df5e1` with BASE. Its tree equals
   `git merge-tree --write-tree 22df5e1 eac0704` exactly, so Task 10 Step 2 was skipped. The plan's
   stop rule for a new commit on a file-structure file fired on a literal reading, and the user
   ruled to continue.
2. **The epoch-0 gate.** The plan's mid-run check could not run, because the sync defers a run
   whose files change every epoch. The gate (CUDA, `bf16-mixed`, geometry, dimension and the
   monitor's distance for the geometry) ran after each seed-1 run instead, followed by a read-only
   spot check over SSH of the first run's files. The gate then ran after every run.
3. **The second source.** `remote finish` slowed with every run on one instance: from 8.7 min
   after training ended for euclidean-d16 seed 2 to 14.6 min for spherical-d16 seed 3, about 1.2
   min more per run. A Mac-side profile found the cause. The final pull treats every file on the
   instance as a replacement, and the promotion step re-hashed the whole cumulative baseline once
   per replacement, so the cost grew with the number of runs on the instance. SOURCE 2 verifies
   the baseline once after the last replacement; a regression test fails on the old code. The first
   run on SOURCE 2 took 1.6 min and the next 1.2 min. The user chose the fix as a private commit.
   The user then sent a new instance, I2, and the remaining 22 runs trained there.
4. **Hyperbolic-d8 seed 3.** It finished training on I1 on 2026-10-10 at 05:24Z, but the driver
   halted when `remote finish` stayed busy for all 20 retries. The run was not relaunched. Its
   `finish` ran about 13:58Z with SOURCE's code, and the local and remote `last.ckpt` SHA-256
   agreed, as for every run.

## 3. The decision

The decision record is `~/naics-artifacts/records/stage8/decision.json`, SHA-256
`601a8e52e5594ae6a1cfb7c9069eb94b01add30c196b8069a6ecab0b04b593fa`. Its question is "Which geometry and dimension should the text stage use (Req 12)?" It resampled the stored per-unit values
with 10000 bootstrap replicates, bootstrap seed 20260924, a minimum of 5 seeds, and interval levels 0.95 (non-inferiority) and 0.9833333333333333 (superiority). It read no panel and logged nothing: the selection log held 157 records
before and after. Each A-against-B comparison adopts A when A is non-inferior on all three panels
and superior on at least one (D8). The 72 comparisons are every ordered pair of the nine arms.
33 of the 72 are adopted.

The adoption matrix: a row's arm is adopted over a column's arm where the cell says `adopted`.

**Result.** The non-dominated set is `['euclidean-d16']` and dominance did not cycle. The tie
order over that set is `['euclidean-d16']`, so no tie needed breaking. The chosen cell is
`euclidean-d16`. Each arm's held-out gain is its point estimate on the held-out regressor panel of
the `covariates+ancestors` MSE minus the arm's `covariates+embedding` MSE. D11 uses it only after
fewer components, lower dimension and non-hyperbolic geometry, and here it broke no tie:

| Arm | Held-out gain |
|---|---|
| euclidean-d16 | 0.01052155742888461 |
| spherical-d16 | -0.005035866372685287 |
| hyperbolic-d8 | -0.02729567488287433 |
| hyperbolic-d32 | 0.0018269504560910164 |
| euclidean-d8 | -0.011175643804330395 |
| euclidean-d32 | 0.007884503525249392 |
| spherical-d8 | -0.019109148979211017 |
| spherical-d32 | 0.004809760717932669 |
| reference | 0.005002948986867558 |

**Reading the result against Req 12.** Req 12 asked whether a hyperbolic advantage appears at low
dimension. At a matched dimension the record shows one comparison in the hyperbolic arm's favor,
hyperbolic-d8 over spherical-d8. Against the Euclidean arm it shows none: at each of d = 8, 16 and
32 the Euclidean arm is adopted over the hyperbolic arm, and the reverse is not adopted. At d = 16
neither the reference nor spherical-d16 is adopted over the other, and at d = 32 spherical-d32 is
adopted over hyperbolic-d32. Across dimensions the record has four more adoptions of a hyperbolic
arm over a flat one, each over a lower-dimension arm: hyperbolic-d32 over euclidean-d8,
spherical-d16 and spherical-d8, and the reference over spherical-d8. These compare dimensions as
much as geometries. In all, five comparisons adopt a hyperbolic arm over a flat arm and eleven
adopt a flat arm over a hyperbolic one.

| Dimension | Hyperbolic arm | Flat arm | Hyperbolic adopted over flat | Flat adopted over hyperbolic |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 8 | hyperbolic-d8 | euclidean-d8 | False | True |
| 8 | hyperbolic-d8 | spherical-d8 | True | False |
| 16 | reference | euclidean-d16 | False | True |
| 16 | reference | spherical-d16 | False | False |
| 32 | hyperbolic-d32 | euclidean-d32 | False | True |
| 32 | hyperbolic-d32 | spherical-d32 | False | True |

The record bounds this reading. The reference has ten seeds and each new arm five, and every
margin comes from the reference's own spread. The 40-epoch budget bound the three arms named in
§1, so a longer budget could move their standing and the record cannot say how. The d = 32 arms
of every geometry peaked early, for a reason this campaign did not examine. The statistics are
validation-panel reads of each seed's selected checkpoint, and nothing here extends beyond them.

## 4. Radius and inert terms

All 40 `tools radius-report` commands exited 0 and reported `passed: true`, with no failed check
and no inert terms. Each read its run's saved first batch and exported table on the CPU and read
no panel; the selection log stayed at 157 records. The flat arms have no radial term and no radius
check (P11), and every one passed on its four terms and learned scales: task, code-code and the
two logit scales, each with a finite positive gradient norm.

The 10 hyperbolic runs passed every Stage 7 check. The least absolute anchor-radius gradient was
positive for every seed, every level 2–6 radius SD was far above 10⁻³ (the least was 0.160366),
and all 20 sector radii were positive and pairwise distinct (the smallest gap was 0.000346045).
The manifold residual and the maximum relative float32 error stayed within the tolerances Stage
7's finding records: the residual at most 1.09139e-11 and the error at most 1.96556e-06. All five
terms and scales had nonzero gradient.

## 5. The selection log

Task 14's check read every campaign log record and every monitor record. There are 10583
log records across 54 physical logs (the Mac's and every instance session's, and an
instance's log carries its earlier sessions' reads too) and 797 monitor records, which is the 40
runs' epochs summed. Every one is a validation read, and none opens a split. The Mac's log held 37
records before the first sweep and 157 after it, so the eight sweeps added exactly 120, fifteen
each. The logs are gitignored, so the lines below are their committed record.

## 7. What later stages read

**Stage 9** reads the chosen cell, `euclidean-d16` (geometry Euclidean, dimension 16), as the
reference for the backbone and one-factor ablations. It reads the decision record at
`~/naics-artifacts/records/stage8/decision.json`, Stage 7's margins unchanged, and keeps `uv.lock`
frozen at `4167042e8a5a8caa9af62973151f681fffb50afaa1a7f6d1f801bd9e58bdac21`. The chosen cell is
not the hyperbolic default that `conf/config.yaml` and the project description name, so the
roadmap stamp and the docs must say which geometry the reference is.

**Stages 10–11.** The chosen cell is flat, and the HGCN feeder refuses a flat arm (P10), so the
graph stage's next resume must say how its arms read a flat text arm.

**Stage 12** consumes the monitor reads, which travel in `SeedRun.monitor_records`.

## Reproduction

Use the pinned canonical inputs, the cached backbone revision, the frozen lock and the recorded
local sources. These commands describe the approved sequential workflow. A completed run's
directory must not be relaunched fresh, so a new reproduction needs new experiment names. The
public finding branch has neither the six private commits nor the machine-specific configuration,
and runs no campaign command. Lifecycle launch and termination of the instances were operator
controlled. `<g>`, `<d>` and `<s>` are a cell's geometry, dimension and seed; `<kkk>` is the
selected epoch in three digits.

```bash
HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 uv run --locked --no-sync naics-embedder remote up --host ubuntu@<IP> seed=<s> experiment_name=stage8-<g>-d<d>-s<s> model.geometry=<g> model.dimension=<d> supervision.manifest_path=data/supervision/stage3-supervision-v2/301cce28-539c-42ea-8781-496bbdcf511c/manifest.json
HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 uv run --locked --no-sync naics-embedder remote train seed=<s> experiment_name=stage8-<g>-d<d>-s<s> model.geometry=<g> model.dimension=<d> supervision.manifest_path=data/supervision/stage3-supervision-v2/301cce28-539c-42ea-8781-496bbdcf511c/manifest.json
HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 uv run --locked --no-sync naics-embedder remote train --resume seed=<s> experiment_name=stage8-<g>-d<d>-s<s> model.geometry=<g> model.dimension=<d> supervision.manifest_path=data/supervision/stage3-supervision-v2/301cce28-539c-42ea-8781-496bbdcf511c/manifest.json
uv run --locked --no-sync naics-embedder remote sync --once
uv run --locked --no-sync naics-embedder remote status
uv run --locked --no-sync naics-embedder remote finish
HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 uv run --locked --no-sync naics-embedder tools sweep --runs 'checkpoints/stage8-<g>-d<d>-s{seed}' --seed 1 --seed 2 --seed 3 --seed 4 --seed 5 --name <g>-d<d> --text-only checkpoints/plan9_exit/text_only.parquet --store ~/naics-artifacts --output ~/naics-artifacts/records/stage8/<g>-d<d>.json --purpose 'Stage 8 geometry-by-dimension sweep: <g>-d<d>, 5 seeds (Req 12)' model.geometry=<g> model.dimension=<d> supervision.manifest_path=data/supervision/stage3-supervision-v2/301cce28-539c-42ea-8781-496bbdcf511c/manifest.json
HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 uv run --locked --no-sync naics-embedder tools radius-report --checkpoint 'checkpoints/stage8-<g>-d<d>-s<s>/epoch=<kkk>.ckpt' --table 'checkpoints/stage8-<g>-d<d>-s<s>/arm_table_epoch=<kkk>.parquet' --output checkpoints/stage8-<g>-d<d>-s<s>/radius_report.json supervision.manifest_path=data/supervision/stage3-supervision-v2/301cce28-539c-42ea-8781-496bbdcf511c/manifest.json
uv run --locked --no-sync naics-embedder tools diagnostics --table 'checkpoints/stage8-<g>-d<d>-s<s>/arm_table_epoch=<kkk>.parquet' --geometry <g> --codebook data/supervision/stage3-supervision-v2/301cce28-539c-42ea-8781-496bbdcf511c/naics_codebook.parquet --output checkpoints/stage8-<g>-d<d>-s<s>/diagnostics.json
uv run --locked --no-sync naics-embedder tools decide --arm ~/naics-artifacts/records/stage8/euclidean-d16.json --arm ~/naics-artifacts/records/stage8/spherical-d16.json --arm ~/naics-artifacts/records/stage8/hyperbolic-d8.json --arm ~/naics-artifacts/records/stage8/hyperbolic-d32.json --arm ~/naics-artifacts/records/stage8/euclidean-d8.json --arm ~/naics-artifacts/records/stage8/euclidean-d32.json --arm ~/naics-artifacts/records/stage8/spherical-d8.json --arm ~/naics-artifacts/records/stage8/spherical-d32.json --arm ~/naics-artifacts/records/stage7/reference.json --margins ~/naics-artifacts/records/stage7/margins.json --name stage8-geometry-by-dimension --question 'Which geometry and dimension should the text stage use (Req 12)?' --store ~/naics-artifacts --output ~/naics-artifacts/records/stage8/decision.json
shasum -a 256 uv.lock ~/naics-artifacts/records/stage8/*.json
```

Run the 40 runs one at a time, finishing and verifying each pull before the next run. The sweep of
a cell checks every seed before its first read, so a refusal logs nothing. Run the cells in the
order of §1's table. Each sweep must add exactly 15 records to the Mac log, and the eight must
add 120. A run that has to move to another instance restores the whole coherent checkpoint and
history set under the same absolute directory and user, then continues only with
`remote train --resume`, keeping seed, settings and budget exact; never resume a finished run to
extend it. CUDA training is not bitwise reproducible, so a rerun's checkpoints, selected epochs,
statistics and decision may differ, even with the same locked inputs and settings.
